Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the surfaces space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 368 and the convergence rate is O(530/n^4).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the surfaces space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 368 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 530. QED.
